! TABLE B1: CATALOGUE OF DENSE CORES IN L1495 
!
! Published in Marsh, K. A. et al. (2016 - arXiv:1602.03143): "A catalogue of dense cores in the Taurus L1495 cloud from the Herschel Gould Belt Survey"
!
! "First-generation" product from the Herschel Gould Belt survey (HGBS - Andre et al. 2010, A&A, 518, L102)
!
! Acknowledgements: If you use this HGBS product for a publication, please refer to the above two papers (Marsh et al. 2016 and Andre et al. 2010).
!
!
! Description of columns:
!
! (1)      Source number.
! (2)      Source name. The full name consists of the HGBS_J prefix followed by the tabulated label derived from J2000 sexagesimal coordinates.
! (3)      Source right ascension (J2000}
! (4)      Source declination (J2000)
! (5)      Sig_70: Detection significance from monochromatic single scales in the image at the specified wavelength. Non-detections receive a value of 0.0
! (6-7)    (I_70)peak: Estimate of the peak intensity and its uncertainty [Jy/beam].
! (8)      (I_70)peak/Ibg: Contrast of the peak intensity over the local background.
! (9)      (I_70)conv,500: Peak intensity after smoothing to a resolution corresponding to the 500 micron beam [Jy/beam].
! (10-11)  S_70: Estimate of the total flux and its uncertainty [Jy].
! (12)     a_70: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along major axis [arcsec].
! (13)     b_70: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along minor axis [arcsec].
! (14)     PA_70: Position angle of source major axis, east of north [deg].
! (15)     Sig_160: Detection significance from monochromatic single scales in the image at the specified wavelength. Non-detections receive a value of 0.0
! (16-17)  (I_160)peak: Estimate of the peak intensity and its uncertainty [Jy/beam].
! (18)     (I_160)peak/Ibg: Contrast of the peak intensity over the local background.
! (19)     (I_160)conv,500: Peak intensity after smoothing to a resolution corresponding to the 500 micron beam [Jy/beam].
! (20-21)  S_160: Estimate of the total flux and its uncertainty [Jy].
! (22)     a_160: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along major axis [arcsec].
! (23)     b_160: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along minor axis [arcsec].
! (24)     PA_160: Position angle of source major axis, east of north [deg].
! (25)     Sig_250: Detection significance from monochromatic single scales in the image at the specified wavelength. Non-detections receive a value of 0.0
! (26-27)  (I_250)peak: Estimate of the peak intensity and its uncertainty [Jy/beam].
! (28)     (I_250)peak/Ibg: Contrast of the peak intensity over the local background.
! (29)     (I_250)conv,500: Peak intensity after smoothing to a resolution corresponding to the 500 micron beam [Jy/beam].
! (30-31)  S_250: Estimate of the total flux and its uncertainty [Jy].
! (32)     a_250: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along major axis [arcsec].
! (33)     b_250: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along minor axis [arcsec].
! (34)     PA_250: Position angle of source major axis, east of north [deg].
! (35)     Sig_350: Detection significance from monochromatic single scales in the image at the specified wavelength. Non-detections receive a value of 0.0
! (36-37)  (I_350)peak: Estimate of the peak intensity and its uncertainty [Jy/beam].
! (38)     (I_350)peak/Ibg: Contrast of the peak intensity over the local background.
! (39)     (I_350)conv,500: Peak intensity after smoothing to a resolution corresponding to the 500 micron beam [Jy/beam].
! (40-41)  S_350: Estimate of the total flux and its uncertainty [Jy].
! (42)     a_350: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along major axis [arcsec].
! (43)     b_350: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along minor axis [arcsec].
! (44)     PA_350: Position angle of source major axis, east of north [deg].
! (45)     Sig_500: Detection significance from monochromatic single scales in the image at the specified wavelength. Non-detections receive a value of 0.0
! (46-47)  (I_500)peak: Estimate of the peak intensity and its uncertainty [Jy/beam].
! (48)     (I_500)peak/Ibg: Contrast of the peak intensity over the local background.
! (49-50)  S_500: Estimate of the total flux and its uncertainty Jy].
! (51)     a_500: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along major axis [arcsec].
! (52)     b_500: Estimate of source size (FWHM) along minor axis [arcsec].
! (53)     PA_500: Position angle of source major axis, east of north [deg].
! (54)     Sig(N_H2)): Detection significance from single scales on high-res column density map
! (55)     (N_H2)peak: Peak column density at 18.2" resolution, estimated by getsources [10^21 cm^-2].
! (56)     (N_H2)peak/(N_H2)bg: Contrast of peak column density over the local background.
! (57)     (N_H2)conv,500: Peak column density estimated by getsources after smoothing to a resolution corresponding to the 500 micron beam [10^21 cm^-2].
! (58)     (N_H2)bg: Column density of local background [10^21 cm^-2].
! (59)     a_(N_H2): Estimate of source size (FWHM) along major axis in column density map [arcsec].
! (60)     b_(N_H2): Estimate of source size (FWHM) along minor axis in column density map [arcsec].
! (61)     PA_(N_H2): Position angle of source major axis in column density map [deg].
! (62)     N_SED: Number of bands in which the source is significant (Sig_lambda > 5)
! (63)     CSAR flag, equal to 1 if source found independently by CSAR, and 0 otherwise.
! (64)     Core type: 1=unbound starless, 2=prestellar, 3=candidate prestellar (non-robust), 4=dense core with embedded protostar.
! (65)     SIMBAD association: ID of nearest match if within 1 arcmin of Herschel position.
! (66)     Comments.
! 
! Key to reference abbreviations used in the SIMBAD association column:
!     BDB2003     Bica, E. et al. 2003, A&A, 397, 177
!     DOBASHI     Dobashi, K. 2011, PASJ, 63, 1
!     GBA2007     Guedel, M. et al. 2007, A&A, 468, 353
!     GMM2009     Gutermuth, R. et al. 2009, ApJS, 184, 18
!     JH          Jones, B. & Herbig, G. 1979, AJ, 84, 1872
!     LM99        Lee, C. & Myers, P. 1999, ApJS, 123, 233
!     LR98        Luhman, K. & Rieke, G. 1998, ApJ, 497, 354
!     MOH94       Mizano, A. et al. 1994, Nature, 368, 719
!     OMK96       Onishi, T. et al. 1996, ApJ, 465, 815
!     OMK2002     Onishi, T. et al. 2002, ApJ, 575, 950
!     SKQ2010     Schmalzl, M. et al. 2010, ApJ, 725, 1327
!     SS94        Strom, K. & Strom, S. 1994, ApJ, 424, 237
!     TGU         Tokyo Gakugei University catalog; Dobashi, K. 2005, PASJ, 57, 1
!     WMD94       Wood, D. et al. 1994, ApJS, 95, 475
!     XEST        XMM-Newton extended survey of the Taurus Molecular Cloud; Guedel, M. et al. 2007, A&A, 468, 353
!     BDB2003     Bica, E. et al. 2003, A&A, 397, 177
!
! Note: For a small number of objects, the reliability of the 250 micron properties may be affected by the presence of pixel dropouts (glitches) visible in the summary card cutouts.
!       The affected objects are indicated by the comment "Possible contamination at 250 microns".
!
